FleetHarbor Logistics - Knowledge Base (plain text)
Total entries: 2194
Each entry starts with [ID] and a title line, then the content, then the tags.

[CO-001] About FleetHarbor Logistics
FleetHarbor Logistics is a third-party logistics (3PL) provider offering warehousing, freight booking, last-mile delivery and shipment tracking for manufacturers and retail clients in western India. Operations run from three distribution warehouses in Ahmedabad, Vadodara and Rajkot.
Tags: company;about;3PL;warehouses

[CO-002] Service levels
Standard delivery windows are 2 to 4 business days for intra-state shipments and 4 to 7 business days for inter-state shipments. Priority shipments are flagged at booking and receive daily status checks from the Logistics Coordinator.
Tags: sla;delivery time;priority

[LW-001] Order lifecycle
Every order moves through six stages: Order Created, Order Confirmed, Picked, Packed, Dispatched and Delivered. An exception at any stage is logged as a hold with a reason code and does not cancel the order.
Tags: workflow;stages;lifecycle

[LW-002] Roles in the workflow
Clients place orders through the client portal or API. Logistics Coordinators validate and book shipments, Warehouse Managers oversee picking, packing and dispatch, and Delivery Executives complete last-mile delivery and proof of delivery.
Tags: roles;responsibilities

[SP-001] Shipment booking
A Logistics Coordinator books each confirmed order within 2 hours. A booking needs the pickup address, consignee address, weight, dimensions, number of cartons and declared value. The LR number is generated at booking.
Tags: booking;LR;documents

[SP-002] Carrier selection
Carriers are chosen by lane rate, service level and available capacity, with preferred carriers used first. Hazardous goods can only move with a carrier holding a valid hazmat certificate.
Tags: carrier;hazmat;rate

[SP-003] Shipping documents
Every dispatch needs an invoice and a packing list. An e-way bill is required for consignments valued above INR 50,000, as per applicable GST rules. Dispatch is blocked in the system until the mandatory documents are attached.
Tags: documents;e-way bill;GST;compliance

[WO-001] Receiving inbound goods
Inbound goods are checked against the Advance Shipping Notice (ASN). Any unit discrepancy above 2 percent must be reported to the Warehouse Manager within 4 hours of receipt, with photos and the ASN reference.
Tags: receiving;ASN;discrepancy

[WO-002] Storage zones
Fast-moving SKUs are stored in Zone A near the dispatch bays. Hazardous items are stored only in Zone H. Every bin location is recorded in the Warehouse Management System (WMS) at put-away.
Tags: storage;zones;WMS;hazmat

[WO-003] Picking and packing
Perishable SKUs are picked using FEFO (First Expiry, First Out) and all other SKUs using FIFO (First In, First Out). Every carton is weighed and labelled before it leaves the packing station.
Tags: picking;FEFO;FIFO;packing

[OT-001] How to track an order
Customers can track a shipment with their order ID or LR number on the client portal, or by calling support. In-transit shipments refresh every 30 minutes.
Tags: tracking;portal;support

[OT-002] Tracking identifiers
Order IDs begin with ORD- and LR numbers begin with LR-. After booking, use the LR number to track, because it is the identifier shared with the carrier.
Tags: identifier;LR;order ID

[DP-001] Delivery attempts
A Delivery Executive can make up to three delivery attempts. Each failed attempt is recorded with a reason code. After the third failed attempt, the shipment returns to the origin hub.
Tags: attempt;failed delivery;return

[DP-002] Proof of delivery
Proof of delivery (POD) must include the receiver's name, a signature or OTP confirmation, and a timestamp. Photos are mandatory for fragile goods.
Tags: POD;signature;OTP;fragile

[DP-003] Delivery hours
Deliveries run between 9:00 AM and 7:00 PM, Monday to Saturday. Deliveries outside these hours need written approval from the Operations Manager and the client.
Tags: delivery hours;schedule

[FAQ-001] Where is my order?
Check the tracking page with your order ID. If the status has not changed in 24 hours, contact the Logistics Coordinator for your lane.
Tags: FAQ;tracking;delay

[FAQ-002] Changing the delivery address
Address changes are accepted only before dispatch. After dispatch, a change must be requested through the carrier and may attract a re-routing fee.
Tags: FAQ;address change;fee

[FAQ-003] Damaged goods on arrival
Note the damage on the POD at delivery, then raise a claim within 7 days with photos and the invoice. See PO-001 for liability limits.
Tags: FAQ;damage;claim

[FAQ-004] Same-day dispatch cutoff
Orders confirmed by 1:00 PM are dispatched the same day from the originating warehouse. Later orders are dispatched the next working day.
Tags: FAQ;cutoff;dispatch

[PO-001] Damage and claims policy
Claims must be filed within 7 days of delivery. Carrier liability is limited to the declared value of the goods unless extended cover was purchased at booking.
Tags: policy;claims;liability;insurance

[PO-002] Returns policy
Returns are accepted within 10 days of delivery for unused goods in original packaging. Return freight is charged to the client unless the return results from a FleetHarbor error.
Tags: policy;returns;freight

[PO-003] Data and privacy
Consignee contact details are used only for delivery and are removed from active systems 90 days after proof of delivery. Consignee personal data must not be shared with third parties.
Tags: privacy;data retention

[PO-004] Escalation matrix
Level 1 is the Logistics Coordinator, Level 2 the Operations Manager and Level 3 the Head of Operations. Escalate a priority shipment to the next level if it has no resolution within 4 hours.
Tags: escalation;priority;contacts

[SS-001] Status: Booked
The shipment has an LR number and an assigned carrier but has not yet been picked up.
Tags: status;booked

[SS-002] Status: In Transit
The carrier has picked up the shipment and it is moving between hubs.
Tags: status;transit

[SS-003] Status: Out for Delivery
The shipment has reached the destination hub or is with the Delivery Executive for delivery today.
Tags: status;out for delivery

[SS-004] Status: Delayed / On Hold
The shipment is delayed because of weather, missing documents or capacity limits. The reason code is shown on the tracking page.
Tags: status;delayed;on hold;reason code

[SS-005] Status: Delivered, Failed Attempt, Returned
Delivered means POD is captured. Failed Attempt means a delivery was tried but not completed. Returned means the shipment is on its way back to origin.
Tags: status;delivered;failed attempt;returned

[TM-001] LR (Lorry Receipt)
A transport document issued by the carrier as proof that goods have been booked for transport. It carries the LR number used for tracking.
Tags: terminology;LR

[TM-002] FEFO and FIFO
FEFO means First Expiry, First Out and FIFO means First In, First Out. These are the two picking methods used in the warehouse.
Tags: terminology;picking

[TM-003] ASN and POD
An Advance Shipping Notice (ASN) is sent by the supplier before goods arrive. Proof of Delivery (POD) is the signed record that goods reached the consignee.
Tags: terminology;ASN;POD

[TM-004] 3PL, LTL and FTL
3PL is third-party logistics. LTL (less-than-truckload) is a shared truck for smaller loads, and FTL (full-truckload) is a truck dedicated to one shipment.
Tags: terminology;3PL;LTL;FTL

[LN-0001] Ahmedabad to Vadodara: delivery window
Shipments from Ahmedabad to Vadodara (Gujarat) are an intra-state movement within Gujarat. The standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days, as set out in the service levels. Priority shipments on this lane are flagged at booking and get daily status checks from the Logistics Coordinator.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;vadodara;intra-state;delivery time;sla;priority

[LN-0002] Ahmedabad to Vadodara: booking and dispatch cutoff
A Logistics Coordinator books each Ahmedabad to Vadodara order within 2 hours of confirmation. Orders confirmed by 1:00 PM are dispatched the same day from Ahmedabad, and later orders are dispatched on the next working day.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;vadodara;intra-state;booking;cutoff;dispatch

[LN-0003] Ahmedabad to Vadodara: documents needed
A Ahmedabad to Vadodara dispatch needs a commercial invoice, a packing list and the LR number generated at booking. Consignments valued above INR 50,000 also need an e-way bill under GST rules. Because this movement stays within Gujarat, the consignee address on the invoice must match the delivery address. Dispatch stays blocked until the mandatory documents are attached.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;vadodara;intra-state;documents;e-way bill;GST

[LN-0004] Ahmedabad to Vadodara: delivery and proof of delivery
Shipments from Ahmedabad to Vadodara are delivered in Vadodara between 9:00 AM and 7:00 PM, Monday to Saturday. Up to three delivery attempts are made, and each failed attempt is logged with a reason code. The proof of delivery must show the receiver's name, a signature or OTP confirmation and a timestamp.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;vadodara;intra-state;delivery hours;POD;attempts

[LN-0005] Ahmedabad to Vadodara: delays and escalation
If a Ahmedabad to Vadodara shipment is delayed or on hold, the tracking page shows a reason code such as weather, missing documents or capacity limits. A priority shipment moves to the next escalation level if it has no resolution within 4 hours.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;vadodara;intra-state;delay;hold;escalation

[LC-0001] Sending electronic goods from Ahmedabad to Vadodara
Keep units in their original boxes with anti-static packing, and never stack heavy cartons on electronics. This Ahmedabad to Vadodara lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. For a damage claim, photograph the carton and the unit, and keep the serial number from the invoice.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;vadodara;intra-state;commodity;electronic-goods

[LC-0002] Sending garments from Ahmedabad to Vadodara
Pack in polybags with a moisture barrier, and keep cartons away from chemicals and food. This Ahmedabad to Vadodara lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. Count pieces against the packing list at delivery and write any shortage on the POD.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;vadodara;intra-state;commodity;garments

[LC-0003] Sending ambient pharmaceuticals from Ahmedabad to Vadodara
Keep batch numbers visible on every carton, store away from direct sunlight and heat, and send the batch certificate with the invoice. This Ahmedabad to Vadodara lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. Record batch and expiry details on the POD, and report any broken tamper seal before signing.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;vadodara;intra-state;commodity;ambient-pharmaceuticals

[LC-0004] Sending perishable food from Ahmedabad to Vadodara
Pick perishables FEFO, and confirm any temperature requirement at booking before dispatch. This Ahmedabad to Vadodara lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. If product is spoiled, note the condition on the POD, and raise a claim with photos and the invoice within 7 days.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;vadodara;intra-state;commodity;perishable-food

[LC-0005] Sending packaged FMCG goods from Ahmedabad to Vadodara
Stack cartons by SKU with labels facing out, respect the stacking limit printed on each carton, and check expiry dates at receiving. This Ahmedabad to Vadodara lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. Note any crushed or wet cartons on the POD and keep them until the claim is closed.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;vadodara;intra-state;commodity;packaged-FMCG-goods

[LC-0006] Sending auto and machinery parts from Ahmedabad to Vadodara
Secure heavy parts with straps or blocking so they do not shift, and mark the weight on each carton. This Ahmedabad to Vadodara lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. Check for shifted or cracked parts at delivery, and record the part numbers on the POD.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;vadodara;intra-state;commodity;auto-and-machinery-parts

[LC-0007] Sending ceramic tiles and glassware from Ahmedabad to Vadodara
Use edge protectors and partition cartons, mark cartons FRAGILE on all sides, and load them upright where possible. This Ahmedabad to Vadodara lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. Photos are mandatory for fragile goods at delivery (see DP-002), so take them before signing.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;vadodara;intra-state;commodity;ceramic-tiles-and-glassware

[LC-0032] Sending perishable food from Ahmedabad to Nadiad

This Ahmedabad to Nadiad lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. If product is spoiled, note the condition on the POD, and raise a claim with photos and the invoice within 7 days.

[LC-0033] Sending packaged FMCG goods from Ahmedabad to Nadiad
Stack cartons by SKU with labels facing out, respect the stacking limit printed on each carton, and check expiry dates at receiving. This Ahmedabad to Nadiad lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. Note any crushed or wet cartons on the POD and keep them until the claim is closed.

[LC-0034] Sending auto and machinery parts from Ahmedabad to Nadiad
Secure heavy parts with straps or blocking so they do not shift, and mark the weight on each carton. This Ahmedabad to Nadiad lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. Check for shifted or cracked parts at delivery, and record the part numbers on the POD.

[LN-0026] Ahmedabad to Bharuch: delivery window
Shipments from Ahmedabad to Bharuch (Gujarat) are an intra-state movement within Gujarat. The standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days, as set out in the service levels. Priority shipments on this lane are flagged at booking and get daily status checks from the Logistics Coordinator.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;bharuch;intra-state;delivery time;sla;priority

[LN-0027] Ahmedabad to Bharuch: booking and dispatch cutoff
A Logistics Coordinator books each Ahmedabad to Bharuch order within 2 hours of confirmation. Orders confirmed by 1:00 PM are dispatched the same day from Ahmedabad, and later orders are dispatched on the next working day.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;bharuch;intra-state;booking;cutoff;dispatch

[LN-0028] Ahmedabad to Bharuch: documents needed
A Ahmedabad to Bharuch dispatch needs a commercial invoice, a packing list and the LR number generated at booking. Consignments valued above INR 50,000 also need an e-way bill under GST rules. Because this movement stays within Gujarat, the consignee address on the invoice must match the delivery address. Dispatch stays blocked until the mandatory documents are attached.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;bharuch;intra-state;documents;e-way bill;GST

[LN-0029] Ahmedabad to Bharuch: delivery and proof of delivery
Shipments from Ahmedabad to Bharuch are delivered in Bharuch between 9:00 AM and 7:00 PM, Monday to Saturday. Up to three delivery attempts are made, and each failed attempt is logged with a reason code. The proof of delivery must show the receiver's name, a signature or OTP confirmation and a timestamp.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;bharuch;intra-state;delivery hours;POD;attempts

If a Ahmedabad to Bharuch shipment is delayed or on hold, the tracking page shows a reason code such as weather, missing documents or capacity limits. A priority shipment moves to the next escalation level if it has no resolution within 4 hours.

Tags: lane;ahmedabad;gandhidham;intra-state;delivery time;sla;priority

[LN-0052] Ahmedabad to Gandhidham: booking and dispatch cutoff
A Logistics Coordinator books each Ahmedabad to Gandhidham order within 2 hours of confirmation. Orders confirmed by 1:00 PM are dispatched the same day from Ahmedabad, and later orders are dispatched on the next working day.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;gandhidham;intra-state;booking;cutoff;dispatch

[LN-0053] Ahmedabad to Gandhidham: documents needed
A Ahmedabad to Gandhidham dispatch needs a commercial invoice, a packing list and the LR number generated at booking. Consignments valued above INR 50,000 also need an e-way bill under GST rules. Because this movement stays within Gujarat, the consignee address on the invoice must match the delivery address. Dispatch stays blocked until the mandatory documents are attached.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;gandhidham;intra-state;documents;e-way bill;GST

[LN-0054] Ahmedabad to Gandhidham: delivery and proof of delivery
Shipments from Ahmedabad to Gandhidham are delivered in Gandhidham between 9:00 AM and 7:00 PM, Monday to Saturday. Up to three delivery attempts are made, and each failed attempt is logged with a reason code. The proof of delivery must show the receiver's name, a signature or OTP confirmation and a timestamp.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;gandhidham;intra-state;delivery hours;POD;attempts

[LN-0055] Ahmedabad to Gandhidham: delays and escalation
If a Ahmedabad to Gandhidham shipment is delayed or on hold, the tracking page shows a reason code such as weather, missing documents or capacity limits. A priority shipment moves to the next escalation level if it has no resolution within 4 hours.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;gandhidham;intra-state;delay;hold;escalation

[LC-0071] Sending electronic goods from Ahmedabad to Gandhidham
Keep units in their original boxes with anti-static packing, and never stack heavy cartons on electronics. This Ahmedabad to Gandhidham lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. For a damage claim, photograph the carton and the unit, and keep the serial number from the invoice.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;gandhidham;intra-state;commodity;electronic-goods

[LC-0072] Sending garments from Ahmedabad to Gandhidham
Pack in polybags with a moisture barrier, and keep cartons away from chemicals and food. This Ahmedabad to Gandhidham lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. Count pieces against the packing list at delivery and write any shortage on the POD.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;gandhidham;intra-state;commodity;garments

[LC-0073] Sending ambient pharmaceuticals from Ahmedabad to Gandhidham
Keep batch numbers visible on every carton, store away from direct sunlight and heat, and send the batch certificate with the invoice. This Ahmedabad to Gandhidham lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. Record batch and expiry details on the POD, and report any broken tamper seal before signing.

[LC-0074] Sending perishable food from Ahmedabad to Gandhidham

This Ahmedabad to Gandhidham lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. If product is spoiled, note the condition on the POD, and raise a claim with photos and the invoice within 7 days.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;gandhidham;intra-state;commodity;perishable-food

[LC-0075] Sending packaged FMCG goods from Ahmedabad to Gandhidham
Stack cartons by SKU with labels facing out, respect the stacking limit printed on each carton, and check expiry dates at receiving. This Ahmedabad to Gandhidham lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. Note any crushed or wet cartons on the POD and keep them until the claim is closed.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;gandhidham;intra-state;commodity;packaged-FMCG-goods

[LC-0076] Sending auto and machinery parts from Ahmedabad to Gandhidham

This Ahmedabad to Gandhidham lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. Check for shifted or cracked parts at delivery, and record the part numbers on the POD.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;gandhidham;intra-state;commodity;auto-and-machinery-parts

[LC-0077] Sending ceramic tiles and glassware from Ahmedabad to Gandhidham

Because this movement stays within Gujarat, the consignee address on the invoice must match the delivery address. Dispatch stays blocked until the mandatory documents are attached.

[LN-0112] Ahmedabad to Nagpur: booking and dispatch cutoff
A Logistics Coordinator books each Ahmedabad to Nagpur order within 2 hours of confirmation. Orders confirmed by 1:00 PM are dispatched the same day from Ahmedabad, and later orders are dispatched on the next working day.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;nagpur;inter-state;booking;cutoff;dispatch

[LN-0113] Ahmedabad to Nagpur: documents needed
A Ahmedabad to Nagpur dispatch needs a commercial invoice, a packing list and the LR number generated at booking. Consignments valued above INR 50,000 also need an e-way bill under GST rules. Because this movement is inter-state, the invoice must show the correct consignee state for GST purposes. Dispatch stays blocked until the mandatory documents are attached.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;nagpur;inter-state;documents;e-way bill;GST

[LN-0114] Ahmedabad to Nagpur: delivery and proof of delivery
Shipments from Ahmedabad to Nagpur are delivered in Nagpur between 9:00 AM and 7:00 PM, Monday to Saturday. Up to three delivery attempts are made, and each failed attempt is logged with a reason code. The proof of delivery must show the receiver's name, a signature or OTP confirmation and a timestamp.

Because this movement is inter-state, the invoice must show the correct consignee state for GST purposes. Dispatch stays blocked until the mandatory documents are attached.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;jodhpur;inter-state;documents;e-way bill;GST

[LN-0139] Ahmedabad to Jodhpur: delivery and proof of delivery
Shipments from Ahmedabad to Jodhpur are delivered in Jodhpur between 9:00 AM and 7:00 PM, Monday to Saturday. Up to three delivery attempts are made, and each failed attempt is logged with a reason code. The proof of delivery must show the receiver's name, a signature or OTP confirmation and a timestamp.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;jodhpur;inter-state;delivery hours;POD;attempts

[LN-0140] Ahmedabad to Jodhpur: delays and escalation
If a Ahmedabad to Jodhpur shipment is delayed or on hold, the tracking page shows a reason code such as weather, missing documents or capacity limits. A priority shipment moves to the next escalation level if it has no resolution within 4 hours.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;jodhpur;inter-state;delay;hold;escalation

[LC-0190] Sending electronic goods from Ahmedabad to Jodhpur

This Ahmedabad to Jodhpur lane is inter-state, so the standard delivery window is 4 to 7 business days from dispatch. For a damage claim, photograph the carton and the unit, and keep the serial number from the invoice.

[LC-0192] Sending ambient pharmaceuticals from Ahmedabad to Jodhpur

[LC-0194] Sending packaged FMCG goods from Ahmedabad to Jodhpur
Stack cartons by SKU with labels facing out, respect the stacking limit printed on each carton, and check expiry dates at receiving. This Ahmedabad to Jodhpur lane is inter-state, so the standard delivery window is 4 to 7 business days from dispatch. Note any crushed or wet cartons on the POD and keep them until the claim is closed.

[LC-0195] Sending auto and machinery parts from Ahmedabad to Jodhpur

This Ahmedabad to Jodhpur lane is inter-state, so the standard delivery window is 4 to 7 business days from dispatch. Check for shifted or cracked parts at delivery, and record the part numbers on the POD.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;jodhpur;inter-state;commodity;auto-and-machinery-parts

[LC-0196] Sending ceramic tiles and glassware from Ahmedabad to Jodhpur

This Ahmedabad to Bhubaneswar lane is inter-state, so the standard delivery window is 4 to 7 business days from dispatch. Record batch and expiry details on the POD, and report any broken tamper seal before signing.

A Logistics Coordinator books each Ahmedabad to Raipur order within 2 hours of confirmation. Orders confirmed by 1:00 PM are dispatched the same day from Ahmedabad, and later orders are dispatched on the next working day.

[LN-0198] Ahmedabad to Raipur: documents needed
A Ahmedabad to Raipur dispatch needs a commercial invoice, a packing list and the LR number generated at booking. Consignments valued above INR 50,000 also need an e-way bill under GST rules. Because this movement is inter-state, the invoice must show the correct consignee state for GST purposes. Dispatch stays blocked until the mandatory documents are attached.
Tags: lane;ahmedabad;raipur;inter-state;documents;e-way bill;GST

Shipments from Ahmedabad to Raipur are delivered in Raipur between 9:00 AM and 7:00 PM, Monday to Saturday. Because this movement stays within Gujarat, the consignee address on the invoice must match the delivery address. Dispatch stays blocked until the mandatory documents are attached.
Tags: lane;vadodara;surat;intra-state;documents;e-way bill;GST

[LN-0224] Vadodara to Surat: delivery and proof of delivery
Shipments from Vadodara to Surat are delivered in Surat between 9:00 AM and 7:00 PM, Monday to Saturday. Up to three delivery attempts are made, and each failed attempt is logged with a reason code. The proof of delivery must show the receiver's name, a signature or OTP confirmation and a timestamp.
Tags: lane;vadodara;surat;intra-state;delivery hours;POD;attempts

[LN-0225] Vadodara to Surat: delays and escalation
If a Vadodara to Surat shipment is delayed or on hold, the tracking page shows a reason code such as weather, missing documents or capacity limits. A priority shipment moves to the next escalation level if it has no resolution within 4 hours.
Tags: lane;vadodara;surat;intra-state;delay;hold;escalation

[LC-0309] Sending electronic goods from Vadodara to Surat
Keep units in their original boxes with anti-static packing, and never stack heavy cartons on electronics. This Vadodara to Surat lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. For a damage claim, photograph the carton and the unit, and keep the serial number from the invoice.
Tags: lane;vadodara;surat;intra-state;commodity;electronic-goods

[LC-0310] Sending garments from Vadodara to Surat
Pack in polybags with a moisture barrier, and keep cartons away from chemicals and food. This Vadodara to Surat lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. Count pieces against the packing list at delivery and write any shortage on the POD.
Tags: lane;vadodara;surat;intra-state;commodity;garments

[LC-0311] Sending ambient pharmaceuticals from Vadodara to Surat
Keep batch numbers visible on every carton, store away from direct sunlight and heat, and send the batch certificate with the invoice. This Vadodara to Surat lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. Record batch and expiry details on the POD, and report any broken tamper seal before signing.
Tags: lane;vadodara;surat;intra-state;commodity;ambient-pharmaceuticals

[LC-0312] Sending perishable food from Vadodara to Surat

This Vadodara to Surat lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. If product is spoiled, note the condition on the POD, and raise a claim with photos and the invoice within 7 days.
Tags: lane;vadodara;surat;intra-state;commodity;perishable-food

[LC-0313] Sending packaged FMCG goods from Vadodara to Surat
Stack cartons by SKU with labels facing out, respect the stacking limit printed on each carton, and check expiry dates at receiving. This Vadodara to Surat lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. Note any crushed or wet cartons on the POD and keep them until the claim is closed.
Tags: lane;vadodara;surat;intra-state;commodity;packaged-FMCG-goods

[LC-0314] Sending auto and machinery parts from Vadodara to Surat
Secure heavy parts with straps or blocking so they do not shift, and mark the weight on each carton. This Vadodara to Surat lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. Check for shifted or cracked parts at delivery, and record the part numbers on the POD.
Tags: lane;vadodara;surat;intra-state;commodity;auto-and-machinery-parts

[LC-0315] Sending ceramic tiles and glassware from Vadodara to Surat
Use edge protectors and partition cartons, mark cartons FRAGILE on all sides, and load them upright where possible. This Vadodara to Surat lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. Photos are mandatory for fragile goods at delivery (see DP-002), so take them before signing.
Tags: lane;vadodara;surat;intra-state;commodity;ceramic-tiles-and-glassware

Pick perishables FEFO, and confirm any temperature requirement at booking before dispatch. This Vadodara to Bhavnagar lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. Note any crushed or wet cartons on the POD and keep them until the claim is closed.

This Vadodara to Bhavnagar lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. Check for shifted or cracked parts at delivery, and record the part numbers on the POD.

[LN-0251] Vadodara to Gandhinagar: delivery window
Shipments from Vadodara to Gandhinagar (Gujarat) are an intra-state movement within Gujarat. The standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days, as set out in the service levels. Priority shipments on this lane are flagged at booking and get daily status checks from the Logistics Coordinator.
Tags: lane;vadodara;gandhinagar;intra-state;delivery time;sla;priority

[LN-0252] Vadodara to Gandhinagar: booking and dispatch cutoff
A Logistics Coordinator books each Vadodara to Gandhinagar order within 2 hours of confirmation. Orders confirmed by 1:00 PM are dispatched the same day from Vadodara, and later orders are dispatched on the next working day.
Tags: lane;vadodara;gandhinagar;intra-state;booking;cutoff;dispatch

[LN-0253] Vadodara to Gandhinagar: documents needed
A Vadodara to Gandhinagar dispatch needs a commercial invoice, a packing list and the LR number generated at booking. Consignments valued above INR 50,000 also need an e-way bill under GST rules. Because this movement stays within Gujarat, the consignee address on the invoice must match the delivery address. Dispatch stays blocked until the mandatory documents are attached.
Tags: lane;vadodara;gandhinagar;intra-state;documents;e-way bill;GST

[LN-0254] Vadodara to Gandhinagar: delivery and proof of delivery
Shipments from Vadodara to Gandhinagar are delivered in Gandhinagar between 9:00 AM and 7:00 PM, Monday to Saturday. Up to three delivery attempts are made, and each failed attempt is logged with a reason code. The proof of delivery must show the receiver's name, a signature or OTP confirmation and a timestamp.
Tags: lane;vadodara;gandhinagar;intra-state;delivery hours;POD;attempts

[LN-0255] Vadodara to Gandhinagar: delays and escalation
If a Vadodara to Gandhinagar shipment is delayed or on hold, the tracking page shows a reason code such as weather, missing documents or capacity limits. A priority shipment moves to the next escalation level if it has no resolution within 4 hours.
Tags: lane;vadodara;gandhinagar;intra-state;delay;hold;escalation

Shipments from Vadodara to Porbandar (Gujarat) are an intra-state movement within Gujarat. The standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days, as set out in the service levels. Priority shipments on this lane are flagged at booking and get daily status checks from the Logistics Coordinator.
Tags: lane;vadodara;porbandar;intra-state;delivery time;sla;priority

[LN-0277] Vadodara to Porbandar: booking and dispatch cutoff
A Logistics Coordinator books each Vadodara to Porbandar order within 2 hours of confirmation. Orders confirmed by 1:00 PM are dispatched the same day from Vadodara, and later orders are dispatched on the next working day.
Tags: lane;vadodara;porbandar;intra-state;booking;cutoff;dispatch

[LN-0278] Vadodara to Porbandar: documents needed
A Vadodara to Porbandar dispatch needs a commercial invoice, a packing list and the LR number generated at booking. Consignments valued above INR 50,000 also need an e-way bill under GST rules. Because this movement stays within Gujarat, the consignee address on the invoice must match the delivery address. Dispatch stays blocked until the mandatory documents are attached.
Tags: lane;vadodara;porbandar;intra-state;documents;e-way bill;GST

[LN-0279] Vadodara to Porbandar: delivery and proof of delivery
Shipments from Vadodara to Porbandar are delivered in Porbandar between 9:00 AM and 7:00 PM, Monday to Saturday. Up to three delivery attempts are made, and each failed attempt is logged with a reason code. The proof of delivery must show the receiver's name, a signature or OTP confirmation and a timestamp.
Tags: lane;vadodara;porbandar;intra-state;delivery hours;POD;attempts

[LN-0280] Vadodara to Porbandar: delays and escalation
If a Vadodara to Porbandar shipment is delayed or on hold, the tracking page shows a reason code such as weather, missing documents or capacity limits. A priority shipment moves to the next escalation level if it has no resolution within 4 hours.
Tags: lane;vadodara;porbandar;intra-state;delay;hold;escalation

[LC-0386] Sending electronic goods from Vadodara to Porbandar
Keep units in their original boxes with anti-static packing, and never stack heavy cartons on electronics. This Vadodara to Porbandar lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. For a damage claim, photograph the carton and the unit, and keep the serial number from the invoice.

[LC-0387] Sending garments from Vadodara to Porbandar

[LC-0388] Sending ambient pharmaceuticals from Vadodara to Porbandar
Keep batch numbers visible on every carton, store away from direct sunlight and heat, and send the batch certificate with the invoice. This Vadodara to Porbandar lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. Record batch and expiry details on the POD, and report any broken tamper seal before signing.

[LC-0389] Sending perishable food from Vadodara to Porbandar

This Vadodara to Porbandar lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. If product is spoiled, note the condition on the POD, and raise a claim with photos and the invoice within 7 days.
Tags: lane;vadodara;porbandar;intra-state;commodity;perishable-food

[LC-0390] Sending packaged FMCG goods from Vadodara to Porbandar
Stack cartons by SKU with labels facing out, respect the stacking limit printed on each carton, and check expiry dates at receiving. This Vadodara to Porbandar lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. Note any crushed or wet cartons on the POD and keep them until the claim is closed.
Tags: lane;vadodara;porbandar;intra-state;commodity;packaged-FMCG-goods

[LC-0391] Sending auto and machinery parts from Vadodara to Porbandar
Secure heavy parts with straps or blocking so they do not shift, and mark the weight on each carton. This Vadodara to Porbandar lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. Check for shifted or cracked parts at delivery, and record the part numbers on the POD.
Tags: lane;vadodara;porbandar;intra-state;commodity;auto-and-machinery-parts

[LC-0392] Sending ceramic tiles and glassware from Vadodara to Porbandar
Use edge protectors and partition cartons, mark cartons FRAGILE on all sides, and load them upright where possible. This Vadodara to Porbandar lane is intra-state, so the standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days from dispatch. Photos are mandatory for fragile goods at delivery (see DP-002), so take them before signing.

[LN-0307] Vadodara to Mumbai: booking and dispatch cutoff
A Logistics Coordinator books each Vadodara to Mumbai order within 2 hours of confirmation. Orders confirmed by 1:00 PM are dispatched the same day from Vadodara, and later orders are dispatched on the next working day.
Tags: lane;vadodara;mumbai;inter-state;booking;cutoff;dispatch

[LN-0308] Vadodara to Mumbai: documents needed
A Vadodara to Mumbai dispatch needs a commercial invoice, a packing list and the LR number generated at booking. Consignments valued above INR 50,000 also need an e-way bill under GST rules. Because this movement is inter-state, the invoice must show the correct consignee state for GST purposes. Dispatch stays blocked until the mandatory documents are attached.
Tags: lane;vadodara;mumbai;inter-state;documents;e-way bill;GST

[LN-0309] Vadodara to Mumbai: delivery and proof of delivery
Shipments from Vadodara to Mumbai are delivered in Mumbai between 9:00 AM and 7:00 PM, Monday to Saturday. Up to three delivery attempts are made, and each failed attempt is logged with a reason code. The proof of delivery must show the receiver's name, a signature or OTP confirmation and a timestamp.
Tags: lane;vadodara;mumbai;inter-state;delivery hours;POD;attempts

Priority shipments on this lane are flagged at booking and get daily status checks from the Logistics Coordinator.
Tags: lane;vadodara;bhopal;inter-state;delivery time;sla;priority

[LN-0332] Vadodara to Bhopal: booking and dispatch cutoff
A Logistics Coordinator books each Vadodara to Bhopal order within 2 hours of confirmation. Orders confirmed by 1:00 PM are dispatched the same day from Vadodara, and later orders are dispatched on the next working day.
Tags: lane;vadodara;bhopal;inter-state;booking;cutoff;dispatch

[LN-0333] Vadodara to Bhopal: documents needed
A Vadodara to Bhopal dispatch needs a commercial invoice, a packing list and the LR number generated at booking. Consignments valued above INR 50,000 also need an e-way bill under GST rules. Because this movement is inter-state, the invoice must show the correct consignee state for GST purposes. Dispatch stays blocked until the mandatory documents are attached.
Tags: lane;vadodara;bhopal;inter-state;documents;e-way bill;GST

[LN-0334] Vadodara to Bhopal: delivery and proof of delivery
Shipments from Vadodara to Bhopal are delivered in Bhopal between 9:00 AM and 7:00 PM, Monday to Saturday. Up to three delivery attempts are made, and each failed attempt is logged with a reason code. The proof of delivery must show the receiver's name, a signature or OTP confirmation and a timestamp.
Tags: lane;vadodara;bhopal;inter-state;delivery hours;POD;attempts

[LN-0335] Vadodara to Bhopal: delays and escalation
If a Vadodara to Bhopal shipment is delayed or on hold, the tracking page shows a reason code such as weather, missing documents or capacity limits. A priority shipment moves to the next escalation level if it has no resolution within 4 hours.
Tags: lane;vadodara;bhopal;inter-state;delay;hold;escalation

[LC-0463] Sending electronic goods from Vadodara to Bhopal
Keep units in their original boxes with anti-static packing, and never stack heavy cartons on electronics. This Vadodara to Bhopal lane is inter-state, so the standard delivery window is 4 to 7 business days from dispatch. For a damage claim, photograph the carton and the unit, and keep the serial number from the invoice.
Tags: lane;vadodara;bhopal;inter-state;commodity;electronic-goods

[LC-0464] Sending garments from Vadodara to Bhopal
Pack in polybags with a moisture barrier, and keep cartons away from chemicals and food. This Vadodara to Bhopal lane is inter-state, so the standard delivery window is 4 to 7 business days from dispatch. Count pieces against the packing list at delivery and write any shortage on the POD.
Tags: lane;vadodara;bhopal;inter-state;commodity;garments

[LC-0465] Sending ambient pharmaceuticals from Vadodara to Bhopal
Keep batch numbers visible on every carton, store away from direct sunlight and heat, and send the batch certificate with the invoice. This Vadodara to Bhopal lane is inter-state, so the standard delivery window is 4 to 7 business days from dispatch. Record batch and expiry details on the POD, and report any broken tamper seal before signing.
Tags: lane;vadodara;bhopal;inter-state;commodity;ambient-pharmaceuticals

[LC-0466] Sending perishable food from Vadodara to Bhopal
Pick perishables FEFO, and confirm any temperature requirement at booking before dispatch. This Vadodara to Bhopal lane is inter-state, so the standard delivery window is 4 to 7 business days from dispatch. If product is spoiled, note the condition on the POD, and raise a claim with photos and the invoice within 7 days.
Tags: lane;vadodara;bhopal;inter-state;commodity;perishable-food

[LC-0467] Sending packaged FMCG goods from Vadodara to Bhopal
Stack cartons by SKU with labels facing out, respect the stacking limit printed on each carton, and check expiry dates at receiving. This Vadodara to Bhopal lane is inter-state, so the standard delivery window is 4 to 7 business days from dispatch. Note any crushed or wet cartons on the POD and keep them until the claim is closed.
Tags: lane;vadodara;bhopal;inter-state;commodity;packaged-FMCG-goods

[LC-0468] Sending auto and machinery parts from Vadodara to Bhopal
Secure heavy parts with straps or blocking so they do not shift, and mark the weight on each carton. This Vadodara to Bhopal lane is inter-state, so the standard delivery window is 4 to 7 business days from dispatch. Check for shifted or cracked parts at delivery, and record the part numbers on the POD.
Tags: lane;vadodara;bhopal;inter-state;commodity;auto-and-machinery-parts

[LC-0469] Sending ceramic tiles and glassware from Vadodara to Bhopal
Use edge protectors and partition cartons, mark cartons FRAGILE on all sides, and load them upright where possible. This Vadodara to Bhopal lane is inter-state, so the standard delivery window is 4 to 7 business days from dispatch. Photos are mandatory for fragile goods at delivery (see DP-002), so take them before signing.
Tags: lane;vadodara;bhopal;inter-state;commodity;ceramic-tiles-and-glassware

Check for shifted or cracked parts at delivery, and record the part numbers on the POD.

[LN-0361] Vadodara to Chandigarh: delivery window

Tags: lane;vadodara;chandigarh;inter-state;delivery time;sla;priority

[LN-0362] Vadodara to Chandigarh: booking and dispatch cutoff
A Logistics Coordinator books each Vadodara to Chandigarh order within 2 hours of confirmation. Orders confirmed by 1:00 PM are dispatched the same day from Vadodara, and later orders are dispatched on the next working day.
Tags: lane;vadodara;chandigarh;inter-state;booking;cutoff;dispatch

[LN-0363] Vadodara to Chandigarh: documents needed
A Vadodara to Chandigarh dispatch needs a commercial invoice, a packing list and the LR number generated at booking. Consignments valued above INR 50,000 also need an e-way bill under GST rules. Because this movement is inter-state, the invoice must show the correct consignee state for GST purposes. Dispatch stays blocked until the mandatory documents are attached.
Tags: lane;vadodara;chandigarh;inter-state;documents;e-way bill;GST

[LN-0364] Vadodara to Chandigarh: delivery and proof of delivery
Shipments from Vadodara to Chandigarh are delivered in Chandigarh between 9:00 AM and 7:00 PM, Monday to Saturday. Up to three delivery attempts are made, and each failed attempt is logged with a reason code. The proof of delivery must show the receiver's name, a signature or OTP confirmation and a timestamp.
Tags: lane;vadodara;chandigarh;inter-state;delivery hours;POD;attempts

[LN-0365] Vadodara to Chandigarh: delays and escalation
If a Vadodara to Chandigarh shipment is delayed or on hold, the tracking page shows a reason code such as weather, missing documents or capacity limits. A priority shipment moves to the next escalation level if it has no resolution within 4 hours.
Tags: lane;vadodara;chandigarh;inter-state;delay;hold;escalation

Shipments from Rajkot to Nadiad (Gujarat) are an intra-state movement within Gujarat. The standard delivery window is 2 to 4 business days, as set out in the service levels. Priority shipments on this lane are flagged at booking and get daily status checks from the Logistics Coordinator.
Tags: lane;rajkot;nadiad;intra-state;delivery time;sla;priority

[LN-0442] Rajkot to Nadiad: booking and dispatch cutoff
A Logistics Coordinator books each Rajkot to Nadiad order within 2 hours of confirmation. Orders confirmed by 1:00 PM are dispatched the same day from Rajkot, and later orders are dispatched on the next working day.
Tags: lane;rajkot;nadiad;intra-state;booking;cutoff;dispatch

[LN-0443] Rajkot to Nadiad: documents needed
A Rajkot to Nadiad dispatch needs a commercial invoice, a packing list and the LR number generated at booking. Consignments valued above INR 50,000 also need an e-way bill under GST rules. Because this movement stays within Gujarat, the consignee address on the invoice must match the delivery address. Dispatch stays blocked until the mandatory documents are attached.
Tags: lane;rajkot;nadiad;intra-state;documents;e-way bill;GST

[LN-0444] Rajkot to Nadiad: delivery and proof of delivery
Shipments from Rajkot to Nadiad are delivered in Nadiad between 9:00 AM and 7:00 PM, Monday to Saturday. Up to three delivery attempts are made, and each failed attempt is logged with a reason code. The proof of delivery must show the receiver's name, a signature or OTP confirmation and a timestamp.
Tags: lane;rajkot;nadiad;intra-state;delivery hours;POD;attempts

[LN-0445] Rajkot to Nadiad: delays and escalation
If a Rajkot to Nadiad shipment is delayed or on hold, the tracking page shows a reason code such as weather, missing documents or capacity limits. A priority shipment moves to the next escalation level if it has no resolution within 4 hours.
Tags: lane;rajkot;nadiad;intra-state;delay;hold;escalation

Tags: lane;rajkot;nashik;inter-state;delivery time;sla;priority

[LN-0527] Rajkot to Nashik: booking and dispatch cutoff
A Logistics Coordinator books each Rajkot to Nashik order within 2 hours of confirmation. Orders confirmed by 1:00 PM are dispatched the same day from Rajkot, and later orders are dispatched on the next working day.
Tags: lane;rajkot;nashik;inter-state;booking;cutoff;dispatch

[LN-0528] Rajkot to Nashik: documents needed
A Rajkot to Nashik dispatch needs a commercial invoice, a packing list and the LR number generated at booking. Consignments valued above INR 50,000 also need an e-way bill under GST rules. Because this movement is inter-state, the invoice must show the correct consignee state for GST purposes. Dispatch stays blocked until the mandatory documents are attached.
Tags: lane;rajkot;nashik;inter-state;documents;e-way bill;GST

[LN-0529] Rajkot to Nashik: delivery and proof of delivery
Shipments from Rajkot to Nashik are delivered in Nashik between 9:00 AM and 7:00 PM, Monday to Saturday. Up to three delivery attempts are made, and each failed attempt is logged with a reason code. The proof of delivery must show the receiver's name, a signature or OTP confirmation and a timestamp.
Tags: lane;rajkot;nashik;inter-state;delivery hours;POD;attempts

[LN-0530] Rajkot to Nashik: delays and escalation
If a Rajkot to Nashik shipment is delayed or on hold, the tracking page shows a reason code such as weather, missing documents or capacity limits. A priority shipment moves to the next escalation level if it has no resolution within 4 hours.
Tags: lane;rajkot;nashik;inter-state;delay;hold;escalation

[LC-0736] Sending electronic goods from Rajkot to Nashik
Keep units in their original boxes with anti-static packing, and never stack heavy cartons on electronics. This Rajkot to Nashik lane is inter-state, so the standard delivery window is 4 to 7 business days from dispatch. For a damage claim, photograph the carton and the unit, and keep the serial number from the invoice.
Tags: lane;rajkot;nashik;inter-state;commodity;electronic-goods

[LC-0737] Sending garments from Rajkot to Nashik
Pack in polybags with a moisture barrier, and keep cartons away from chemicals and food. This Rajkot to Nashik lane is inter-state, so the standard delivery window is 4 to 7 business days from dispatch. Count pieces against the packing list at delivery and write any shortage on the POD.
Tags: lane;rajkot;nashik;inter-state;commodity;garments

[LC-0738] Sending ambient pharmaceuticals from Rajkot to Nashik
Keep batch numbers visible on every carton, store away from direct sunlight and heat, and send the batch certificate with the invoice. This Rajkot to Nashik lane is inter-state, so the standard delivery window is 4 to 7 business days from dispatch. Record batch and expiry details on the POD, and report any broken tamper seal before signing.
Tags: lane;rajkot;nashik;inter-state;commodity;ambient-pharmaceuticals

[LC-0739] Sending perishable food from Rajkot to Nashik
Pick perishables FEFO, and confirm any temperature requirement at booking before dispatch. This Rajkot to Nashik lane is inter-state, so the standard delivery window is 4 to 7 business days from dispatch. If product is spoiled, note the condition on the POD, and raise a claim with photos and the invoice within 7 days.
Tags: lane;rajkot;nashik;inter-state;commodity;perishable-food

[LC-0740] Sending packaged FMCG goods from Rajkot to Nashik
Stack cartons by SKU with labels facing out, respect the stacking limit printed on each carton, and check expiry dates at receiving. This Rajkot to Nashik lane is inter-state, so the standard delivery window is 4 to 7 business days from dispatch. Note any crushed or wet cartons on the POD and keep them until the claim is closed.
Tags: lane;rajkot;nashik;inter-state;commodity;packaged-FMCG-goods

[LC-0741] Sending auto and machinery parts from Rajkot to Nashik
Secure heavy parts with straps or blocking so they do not shift, and mark the weight on each carton. This Rajkot to Nashik lane is inter-state, so the standard delivery window is 4 to 7 business days from dispatch. Check for shifted or cracked parts at delivery, and record the part numbers on the POD.
Tags: lane;rajkot;nashik;inter-state;commodity;auto-and-machinery-parts

[LC-0742] Sending ceramic tiles and glassware from Rajkot to Nashik
Use edge protectors and partition cartons, mark cartons FRAGILE on all sides, and load them upright where possible. This Rajkot to Nashik lane is inter-state, so the standard delivery window is 4 to 7 business days from dispatch. Photos are mandatory for fragile goods at delivery (see DP-002), so take them before signing.

This Rajkot to Udaipur lane is inter-state, so the standard delivery window is 4 to 7 business days from dispatch. Note any crushed or wet cartons on the POD and keep them until the claim is closed.
Tags: lane;rajkot;udaipur;inter-state;commodity;packaged-FMCG-goods

[LC-0776] Sending auto and machinery parts from Rajkot to Udaipur
Secure heavy parts with straps or blocking so they do not shift, and mark the weight on each carton. This Rajkot to Udaipur lane is inter-state, so the standard delivery window is 4 to 7 business days from dispatch. Check for shifted or cracked parts at delivery, and record the part numbers on the POD.

Tags: lane;rajkot;jodhpur;inter-state;booking;cutoff;dispatch

[LN-0558] Rajkot to Jodhpur: documents needed
A Rajkot to Jodhpur dispatch needs a commercial invoice, a packing list and the LR number generated at booking. Consignments valued above INR 50,000 also need an e-way bill under GST rules. Because this movement is inter-state, the invoice must show the correct consignee state for GST purposes. Dispatch stays blocked until the mandatory documents are attached.
Tags: lane;rajkot;jodhpur;inter-state;documents;e-way bill;GST

[LN-0559] Rajkot to Jodhpur: delivery and proof of delivery
Shipments from Rajkot to Jodhpur are delivered in Jodhpur between 9:00 AM and 7:00 PM, Monday to Saturday. Because this movement is inter-state, the invoice must show the correct consignee state for GST purposes. Dispatch stays blocked until the mandatory documents are attached.
Tags: lane;rajkot;bhubaneswar;inter-state;documents;e-way bill;GST

[LN-0614] Rajkot to Bhubaneswar: delivery and proof of delivery
Shipments from Rajkot to Bhubaneswar are delivered in Bhubaneswar between 9:00 AM and 7:00 PM, Monday to Saturday. Up to three delivery attempts are made, and each failed attempt is logged with a reason code. The proof of delivery must show the receiver's name, a signature or OTP confirmation and a timestamp.
Tags: lane;rajkot;bhubaneswar;inter-state;delivery hours;POD;attempts

[LN-0615] Rajkot to Bhubaneswar: delays and escalation
If a Rajkot to Bhubaneswar shipment is delayed or on hold, the tracking page shows a reason code such as weather, missing documents or capacity limits. A priority shipment moves to the next escalation level if it has no resolution within 4 hours.
Tags: lane;rajkot;bhubaneswar;inter-state;delay;hold;escalation

[CM-0001] Packing mobile phones and laptops
Pack mobile phones and laptops in their retail boxes inside a double-walled carton with corner protection. Inspect every carton at the packing station and do not dispatch cartons with crushed corners or open seals.
Tags: commodity;packing;mobile-phones-and-laptops

[CM-0002] Storing mobile phones and laptops in the warehouse
Store mobile phones and laptops in a dry, dust-free bin in Zone A, away from direct sunlight. Record the bin location in the WMS at put-away, as set out in WO-002.
Tags: commodity;storage;mobile-phones-and-laptops

[CM-0003] Labelling mobile phones and laptops
Label mobile phones and laptops with the model number and serial range on the outer carton. Labels must stay readable at the packing station and on the dock.
Tags: commodity;labelling;mobile-phones-and-laptops

[CM-0004] Documents for mobile phones and laptops
Prepare a commercial invoice with IMEI or serial details, and a packing list for every dispatch of mobile phones and laptops. Dispatch stays blocked until the mandatory documents are attached (see SP-003).
Tags: commodity;documents;mobile-phones-and-laptops

[CM-0005] Transporting mobile phones and laptops
The main transit risk for mobile phones and laptops is theft and pilferage. Secure cartons on the vehicle so they cannot shift, and report any change in carton condition at the dock.
Tags: commodity;transport;mobile-phones-and-laptops

[CM-0006] Preventing damage to mobile phones and laptops
To prevent damage to mobile phones and laptops, watch closely for theft and pilferage. Use trolleys or forklifts for heavy cartons, and never throw or drop them during loading.
Tags: commodity;damage;mobile-phones-and-laptops

[CM-0007] Receiving mobile phones and laptops at the warehouse
Check inbound mobile phones and laptops against the ASN and inspect the packaging. Report any unit discrepancy above 2 percent to the Warehouse Manager within 4 hours (see WO-001).
Tags: commodity;receiving;mobile-phones-and-laptops

[CM-0008] Delivering mobile phones and laptops
At delivery, record the condition of each carton of mobile phones and laptops on the POD. Photos are mandatory for fragile goods (see DP-002).
Tags: commodity;delivery;mobile-phones-and-laptops

Prepare an invoice and a packing list, plus the ingredient sheet if the client supplies one for every dispatch of cosmetics and personal care. Dispatch stays blocked until the mandatory documents are attached (see SP-003).
Tags: commodity;documents;cosmetics-and-personal-care

[CM-0157] Transporting cosmetics and personal care
The main transit risk for cosmetics and personal care is leakage and heat damage. Secure cartons on the vehicle so they cannot shift, and report any change in carton condition at the dock.
Tags: commodity;transport;cosmetics-and-personal-care

[CM-0158] Preventing damage to cosmetics and personal care
To prevent damage to cosmetics and personal care, watch closely for leakage and heat damage. Use trolleys or forklifts for heavy cartons, and never throw or drop them during loading.
Tags: commodity;damage;cosmetics-and-personal-care

[CM-0159] Receiving cosmetics and personal care at the warehouse
Check inbound cosmetics and personal care against the ASN and inspect the packaging. Report any unit discrepancy above 2 percent to the Warehouse Manager within 4 hours (see WO-001).
Tags: commodity;receiving;cosmetics-and-personal-care

[CM-0160] Delivering cosmetics and personal care
At delivery, record the condition of each carton of cosmetics and personal care on the POD. Photos are mandatory for fragile goods (see DP-002).
Tags: commodity;delivery;cosmetics-and-personal-care

[CM-0161] Packing food grains and pulses
Pack food grains and pulses in clean, stitched and labelled bags with moisture protection. Inspect every carton at the packing station and do not dispatch cartons with crushed corners or open seals.
Tags: commodity;packing;food-grains-and-pulses

Store food grains and pulses on pallets in a dry area, rotated FIFO or FEFO as set. Record the bin location in the WMS at put-away, as set out in WO-002.

[DC-0001] Commercial invoice: what it is
The seller's bill to the buyer, showing the goods, quantities, unit prices and total value. The document travels with the consignment file, so it is kept with the invoice and packing list until the shipment is delivered.
Tags: document;commercial invoice

[DC-0002] Commercial invoice: when it is required
Needed for almost every dispatch, both domestic and export, and used to declare the value. If a required copy is missing, tell the Logistics Coordinator before dispatch so the shipment is not put on hold (see SS-004).
Tags: document;commercial invoice

[DC-0003] Commercial invoice: checks before dispatch
Check that the consignee name, HSN code, quantities and value match the packing list. Record any mismatch in the booking notes and inform the Logistics Coordinator before the vehicle leaves.
Tags: document;commercial invoice

[DC-0004] Packing list: what it is
A document listing each carton's contents, weight and dimensions. The document travels with the consignment file, so it is kept with the invoice and packing list until the shipment is delivered.
Tags: document;packing list

[DC-0005] Packing list: when it is required
Required with every dispatch so the consignee can verify what arrived. If a required copy is missing, tell the Logistics Coordinator before dispatch so the shipment is not put on hold (see SS-004).
Tags: document;packing list

[DC-0006] Packing list: checks before dispatch
Check that the carton count and total weight match the physical cartons. Record any mismatch in the booking notes and inform the Logistics Coordinator before the vehicle leaves.
Tags: document;packing list

[DC-0007] E-way bill: what it is
A GST document, identified by a 12-digit number, that tracks goods moving by road. The document travels with the consignment file, so it is kept with the invoice and packing list until the shipment is delivered.
Tags: document;e-way bill

[DC-0008] E-way bill: when it is required
Required for consignments valued above INR 50,000 under GST rules. If a required copy is missing, tell the Logistics Coordinator before dispatch so the shipment is not put on hold (see SS-004).
Tags: document;e-way bill

[DC-0009] E-way bill: checks before dispatch
Check that the e-way bill is valid for the route and that the vehicle number is correct. Record any mismatch in the booking notes and inform the Logistics Coordinator before the vehicle leaves.
Tags: document;e-way bill

[DC-0010] Delivery challan: what it is
A GST document issued when goods move without a sale, such as for job work or a branch transfer. The document travels with the consignment file, so it is kept with the invoice and packing list until the shipment is delivered.
Tags: document;delivery challan

[DC-0011] Delivery challan: when it is required
Needed when stock moves between locations or goes out for processing. If a required copy is missing, tell the Logistics Coordinator before dispatch so the shipment is not put on hold (see SS-004).
Tags: document;delivery challan

[DC-0012] Delivery challan: checks before dispatch
Check that the challan lists the goods, quantities and purpose of movement. Record any mismatch in the booking notes and inform the Logistics Coordinator before the vehicle leaves.
Tags: document;delivery challan

[DC-0013] Tax invoice: what it is
A GST invoice that shows the tax charged on a supply of goods. The document travels with the consignment file, so it is kept with the invoice and packing list until the shipment is delivered.
Tags: document;tax invoice

[DC-0014] Tax invoice: when it is required
Issued when goods are sold to a registered buyer. If a required copy is missing, tell the Logistics Coordinator before dispatch so the shipment is not put on hold (see SS-004).
Tags: document;tax invoice

[DC-0015] Tax invoice: checks before dispatch
Check the GSTIN, the tax rate and the invoice number. Record any mismatch in the booking notes and inform the Logistics Coordinator before the vehicle leaves.
Tags: document;tax invoice

[DC-0016] Bill of lading: what it is
A sea freight document that acts as a receipt for cargo and as evidence of the shipping contract. The document travels with the consignment file, so it is kept with the invoice and packing list until the shipment is delivered.
Tags: document;bill of lading

[DC-0017] Bill of lading: when it is required
Issued by the ocean carrier for every sea shipment. If a required copy is missing, tell the Logistics Coordinator before dispatch so the shipment is not put on hold (see SS-004).
Tags: document;bill of lading

[DC-0018] Bill of lading: checks before dispatch
Check that the shipper, consignee, notify party and port names match the booking. Record any mismatch in the booking notes and inform the Logistics Coordinator before the vehicle leaves.
Tags: document;bill of lading

[DC-0019] Airway bill: what it is
The air cargo document issued by the airline or forwarder for a consignment. The document travels with the consignment file, so it is kept with the invoice and packing list until the shipment is delivered.
Tags: document;airway bill

[DC-0020] Airway bill: when it is required
Required for every air shipment. If a required copy is missing, tell the Logistics Coordinator before dispatch so the shipment is not put on hold (see SS-004).
Tags: document;airway bill

[DC-0021] Airway bill: checks before dispatch
Check the AWB number, the number of pieces and the chargeable weight. Record any mismatch in the booking notes and inform the Logistics Coordinator before the vehicle leaves.
Tags: document;airway bill

[DC-0022] Certificate of origin: what it is
A document that states the country where the goods were made. The document travels with the consignment file, so it is kept with the invoice and packing list until the shipment is delivered.
Tags: document;certificate of origin

[DC-0023] Certificate of origin: when it is required
Needed for export shipments that claim preferential duty under a trade agreement. If a required copy is missing, tell the Logistics Coordinator before dispatch so the shipment is not put on hold (see SS-004).
Tags: document;certificate of origin

[DC-0024] Certificate of origin: checks before dispatch
Check that the country of origin matches the goods and the invoice. Record any mismatch in the booking notes and inform the Logistics Coordinator before the vehicle leaves.
Tags: document;certificate of origin

Tags: document;dangerous goods declaration

[DC-0074] Dangerous goods declaration: when it is required
Required for every dangerous goods shipment. If a required copy is missing, tell the Logistics Coordinator before dispatch so the shipment is not put on hold (see SS-004).
Tags: document;dangerous goods declaration

[DC-0075] Dangerous goods declaration: checks before dispatch
Check the UN number, hazard class and packing group. Record any mismatch in the booking notes and inform the Logistics Coordinator before the vehicle leaves.
Tags: document;dangerous goods declaration

[DC-0076] ISPM 15 wood packaging mark: what it is
A stamp showing that wooden packaging was heat-treated or fumigated to international standards. The document travels with the consignment file, so it is kept with the invoice and packing list until the shipment is delivered.
Tags: document;ispm 15 wood packaging mark

[DC-0077] ISPM 15 wood packaging mark: when it is required
Needed for export shipments that use wooden pallets or crates. If a required copy is missing, tell the Logistics Coordinator before dispatch so the shipment is not put on hold (see SS-004).
Tags: document;ispm 15 wood packaging mark

[DC-0078] ISPM 15 wood packaging mark: checks before dispatch
Check that the stamp is visible on the wood. Record any mismatch in the booking notes and inform the Logistics Coordinator before the vehicle leaves.
Tags: document;ispm 15 wood packaging mark

[DC-0079] Quality inspection report: what it is
A report from an inspection of goods before shipment. The document travels with the consignment file, so it is kept with the invoice and packing list until the shipment is delivered.
Tags: document;quality inspection report

[DC-0080] Quality inspection report: when it is required
Requested by the buyer or client for high-value or regulated goods. If a required copy is missing, tell the Logistics Coordinator before dispatch so the shipment is not put on hold (see SS-004).
Tags: document;quality inspection report

[DC-0081] Quality inspection report: checks before dispatch
Check the inspection date and the lot or batch covered. Record any mismatch in the booking notes and inform the Logistics Coordinator before the vehicle leaves.
Tags: document;quality inspection report

[DC-0082] Authorisation letter: what it is
A letter that authorises a person or company to collect or clear goods. The document travels with the consignment file, so it is kept with the invoice and packing list until the shipment is delivered.
Tags: document;authorisation letter

[DC-0083] Authorisation letter: when it is required
Needed when someone other than the consignee collects the goods. If a required copy is missing, tell the Logistics Coordinator before dispatch so the shipment is not put on hold (see SS-004).
Tags: document;authorisation letter

[DC-0084] Authorisation letter: checks before dispatch
Check the signatory and the validity date. Record any mismatch in the booking notes and inform the Logistics Coordinator before the vehicle leaves.
Tags: document;authorisation letter

[DC-0085] Delivery order: what it is
A document that authorises release of goods from a warehouse, port or terminal. The document travels with the consignment file, so it is kept with the invoice and packing list until the shipment is delivered.
Tags: document;delivery order

[DC-0086] Delivery order: when it is required
Used when goods leave a warehouse or terminal under a release order. If a required copy is missing, tell the Logistics Coordinator before dispatch so the shipment is not put on hold (see SS-004).
Tags: document;delivery order

[DC-0087] Delivery order: checks before dispatch
Check the order number, consignee and quantities. Record any mismatch in the booking notes and inform the Logistics Coordinator before the vehicle leaves.
Tags: document;delivery order

[DC-0088] Proof of delivery (POD): what it is
The signed record that goods reached the consignee (see TM-003). The document travels with the consignment file, so it is kept with the invoice and packing list until the shipment is delivered.
Tags: document;proof of delivery (pod)

[DC-0089] Proof of delivery (POD): when it is required
Required for every completed delivery. If a required copy is missing, tell the Logistics Coordinator before dispatch so the shipment is not put on hold (see SS-004).
Tags: document;proof of delivery (pod)

[DC-0090] Proof of delivery (POD): checks before dispatch
Check that the receiver's name, signature or OTP and timestamp are captured. Record any mismatch in the booking notes and inform the Logistics Coordinator before the vehicle leaves.
Tags: document;proof of delivery (pod)

The shipment is booked and an LR number is generated for tracking. What to do: Keep the LR number, because the carrier uses it too.

The shipment is booked and an LR number is generated for tracking. What happens next: A pickup is scheduled with the assigned carrier.

[EV-0006] Pickup Scheduled: what happens next
A carrier has been assigned to collect the goods on a set day. What happens next: The carrier collects the goods and the status changes.

The carrier has collected the goods from the shipper. What happens next: The goods move to the origin hub.
